Download browser_launcher_v1.0.0.elf official release

Source and verification

Original native scripts and payload bytes are retained. Authored entry controls require a matching console browser and an explicit Load action; the original host may then prepare or load automatically. Original loader selection and multi-file ordering are preserved. Missing or invalid files are blocked without substituting another firmware or payload.

Physical console execution, TLS and offline cache behaviour remain untested. Some original optional services or external libraries require their original server or Internet connection. This is a local integration, not a claim that upstream defects were repaired. The optional WebActivator payload.js dependencies in 900NM and 900New were quarantined by Windows Defender and are blocked here; they were not restored or bypassed.

Original KARO and embedded-project attributions are retained in their files. Pinned original repository
